I used to be able to sit with something, but lately I’m just bouncing from one thing to another and not finishing what I’m supposed to be doing.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself getting distracted like this in other parts of your life, like at home or while driving, or is it mostly at work?\n\nPatient: It’s… mostly what I notice at work, honestly. Away from work… I’m trying, but I honestly don’t have a great example. My brain keeps going right back to the bench, because that’s where it’s been obvious and kind of scary.

Like, I’ll be in the middle of a customer email, then I notice a saw mark, then a noise outside pulls me away, and next thing I know I’ve lost track of the whole work order. I’m sitting there with the pieces out like, wait, what step was I even on? That’s the thing that made me think something’s not right—my mind just won’t stay on one thing anymore.

Away from work… I’m honestly having trouble landing on a clean example. Like, at home I’ll start watching something and then end up thinking about whether I left a tool out, or I’ll notice some little scratch on the table and get sidetracked, but it’s not as easy to point to.
